# QbAutopost.Api — Implementation Tracker

Updated: 2026-10-28 · Owner: Braj · Executor: Claude Code
Spec: `spec.md` · Plan: `plan.md`

**How to update (Claude Code does this after every task):**
1. Change the task's Status: `todo` → `doing` → `done` (or `blocked` with a note).
2. Fill *Files* (paths created/changed) and *Verified by* (test names or the command run).
3. Append one line to the Session log.
4. Never delete rows; never mark a **[server]** task `done` — leave it `ready-for-human` and the human flips it.

Status legend: `todo` · `doing` · `done` · `blocked` · `ready-for-human`

## M3 — XLSX, PDF, T2, G1

| ID | Task | Spec | Files | Verified by | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| T-301 | XlsxStatementParser (ClosedXML → layout logic) | FR-3 | `src/QbAutopost.Core/Extract/{XlsxGrid,XlsxStatementParser}.cs`, `Extract/StatementGridParser.cs` (`acceptIsoDates`), `Models/HoldReasons.cs` (`unreadable-statement`), `Pipeline/JobPipeline.cs` (`.xlsx` routed; only `.pdf` still `extractor-not-available`), `QbAutopost.Core.csproj` (ClosedXML 0.105.1); tests `Core.Tests/Extract/XlsxStatementParserTests.cs`, `TestSupport/XlsxBuilder.cs`, `Extract/CsvStatementParserTests.cs` (+1), `Pipeline/JobPipelineTests.cs` (+2, pdf hold); fixture `tests/fixtures/statements/chase-checking-4521.xlsx` | `XlsxStatementParserTests` (17), `JobPipelineTests.Should_PostSameLines_When_BankStatementIsAWorkbook`, `…_When_WorkbookIsCorrupt`; `dotnet test` → Core 283, Api 68 | done | First worksheet only, blank rows dropped, first non-blank row = header, same layouts as CSV. Cells read without display formats: numbers → invariant digits (double → decimal keeps 15 digits, so `0.1+0.2` → 0.3; > 2 decimals still a row error), real date cells → `yyyy-MM-dd` (time dropped), booleans/errors → text that fails parsing, formulas → the result saved in the file (never recalculated; none saved → blank → statement held). **Date decision (Q-1 kept, Q-23):** text dates must match the layout format, no invariant fallback; the XLSX parser also accepts exact `yyyy-MM-dd`. A workbook ClosedXML cannot open → `unreadable-statement`. Fixture generated by a throw-away ClosedXML program from the sample bank CSV (synthetic). ClosedXML never writes formula results, so `XlsxBuilder` adds `<v>` to the sheet XML to imitate Excel |
| T-302 | PdfText (PdfPig), scanned detection, Ocr wrapper behind Ocr.Enabled | FR-3 | `src/QbAutopost.Core/Abstractions/IOcr.cs` (`IOcr`, `DisabledOcr`), `Core/Extract/PdfText.cs` (`PdfText`, `PdfTextResult`, `PdfPageText`), `Core/Pipeline/JobPipeline.cs` (new `IOcr` constructor argument; statements read async; PDFs go through `PdfText`), `QbAutopost.Core.csproj` (PdfPig 0.1.16); `src/QbAutopost.Api/Ocr/TesseractOcr.cs`, `Api/Program.cs` (`IOcr` by `Ocr:Enabled`, resolved at startup), `Api/Configuration/AppSettings.cs` (relative `TessDataPath` resolved), `QbAutopost.Api.csproj` (Tesseract 5.2.0); tests `Core.Tests/Extract/PdfTextTests.cs`, `TestSupport/{PdfBuilder,TestImage,FakeOcr}.cs`, `Pipeline/JobPipelineTests.cs` (+3, pdf test now uses a real PDF), `Output/{AnalysisGoldenTests,ResultDocumentTests}.cs` (constructor), `Api.Tests/Api/OcrApiTests.cs` | `PdfTextTests` (12), `JobPipelineTests` PDF cases (4), `OcrApiTests` (3); `dotnet test` → Core 298, Api 71. Manual (Windows, scratch program, tessdata_fast `eng.traineddata`, not committed): real `TesseractOcr` read "08/15/2026 ACH DEBIT UNKNOWN PLUMBER SVC -3199.70" exactly, both from a PNG and from a one-image PDF through `PdfText` | done | Page text via `ContentOrderTextExtractor`; pages joined with `\n\f\n`. Scanned = fewer than 40 **non-whitespace** characters (Q-24). OCR disabled + any scanned page → whole file `scanned-pdf-ocr-disabled` (error names the pages). OCR enabled → each image on a scanned page is OCR'd (JPEG streams passed as-is, other images re-encoded to PNG by PdfPig; an image neither can give → `unreadable-statement`); OCR text is appended to the page's own text. Scanned page with no images → its short text is kept (Q-24). Damaged/encrypted/non-PDF file or OCR exception → `unreadable-statement`; cancellation propagates. Readable PDFs are still held `extractor-not-available` until T-303 (T2). `Ocr:Enabled` with a missing or empty `TessDataPath` stops the host at startup. The Tesseract package bundles Windows x86/x64 native libraries only; Linux needs system Tesseract 5 + Leptonica (OCR is off by default, spec Q3). Only `eng` is loaded |
| T-303 | StatementLlmExtractor T2: prompt, chunking, merge, validation | §9.2 | `src/QbAutopost.Core/Hermes/{prompts/statement.md,StatementAnswer}.cs`, `Core/Extract/StatementLlmExtractor.cs` (`StatementChunk`), `Core/Extract/StatementParseResult.cs` (`Totals`, `StatementTotals`), `Core/Models/HoldReasons.cs` (`hermes-failed`, `extraction-conflict`), `src/QbAutopost.Api/Program.cs` (`statement.md` required at startup); tests `Core.Tests/Hermes/StatementAnswerTests.cs`, `Core.Tests/Extract/StatementLlmExtractorTests.cs`, `TestSupport/ScriptedHermes.cs` (moved out of `HermesSpecReaderTests`); fixture `tests/fixtures/statements/chase-checking-4521.pdf.txt` | `StatementAnswerTests` (27), `StatementLlmExtractorTests` (17, one through the real `HermesClient` retry); `dotnet test` → Core 342, Api 71 | done | Fixture text + `hermes/statement.json` give the same rows and fingerprints as the sample bank CSV (FR-3 AC, T-305). Validation: kind `bank`/`card` and direction `debit`/`credit` (any case), ISO dates (real dates), amounts present, > 0 and ≤ 2 decimals, balances ≤ 2 decimals, `accountLast4` 4 digits or null, period start ≤ end, count ≥ 0, `rows` required (may be empty), `checkNo` a string. Chunking: whole text ≤ 60 000 chars → one request; otherwise consecutive page groups each ≤ 60 000; a page over the limit goes alone, never cut. Each request says "part i of n (pages a-b)". Merge: rows joined in part order, `lineNo` 1…n; kind, last-four, period, balances and count must agree across parts (null = not shown) else `extraction-conflict` (Q-25). File-name last-four wins; a different last-four in the text → `conflicting-last4`; none anywhere → `unknown-account`. Any `HermesException` → `hermes-failed`, remaining parts not sent; cancellation propagates. **Not wired into the pipeline yet** — PDFs stay `extractor-not-available` until T-304 adds the T2 G1 checks, so no T2 row can post unchecked |

T-609 checklist (human):
- [ ] Before starting: `Company:FilePath` points at a **copy** of the company file; QuickBooks is open on that copy in the same Windows session as the API; `QuickBooks:Fake` is not set
- [ ] `.\scripts\qb-server-check.ps1 -Step health` → ok (certificate dialog answered "Yes, always"; user HermesBridge). If it fails with "not registered", the API bitness does not match QuickBooks
- [ ] `-Step sync` → counts plausible; `missingInRules` empty after fixing rules.json; `qb-audit/sync-lists.response.qbxml` looks like a real SDK answer
- [ ] `-Step dryrun -Folder <real folder>` → `ready`; `analysis.json` reviewed
- [ ] `-Step post -JobId <id> -ConfirmCopy` → `posted` or `partial` as expected; transactions visible in QuickBooks with the right account, payee, date, amount and memo
- [ ] Posting sends `query-1…n.qbxml` first: check the `query-*.response.qbxml` files show status 0 or 1 (confirms `DepositQuery` accepts `AccountFilter`, Q-36)
- [ ] `response.qbxml`: each `*AddRs` has `TxnID`, `EditSequence` and `Amount`/`DepositTotal` equal to the statement (G5, T-605)
- [ ] Re-run the same folder with `force=true` and post again → every line skipped `already-posted` (G4 ledger check)
- [ ] G4 live check: on the copy, enter one statement line by hand (same account, date, amount, payee), then dry run and post a folder that contains it → that line is skipped `already-in-quickbooks`; with the date moved by 1–3 days it is held `possible-duplicate`
- [ ] `-Step undo -BatchId '<id>#<n>' -ConfirmCopy` → all deleted; `undo-*.response.qbxml` shows status 0 per `TxnDelRs`; transactions gone in QuickBooks
- [ ] With QuickBooks closed: `-Step health` → 503 with a clear message, and a post ends `partial` "nothing posted"
- [ ] COM constants confirmed (`OpenConnection2` local = 1, `BeginSession` DoNotCare = 2, `QbSession.cs`)
- [x] Bitness confirmed (x64/x86) and recorded here: **x64** — settled on the server 2026-09-20: the app ran as an x64 process, `QBXMLRP2.RequestProcessor` was created and `GET /health/quickbooks` answered `ok: true`, `"Intuit QuickBooks Enterprise Solutions 24.0 34.0"`, company file `D:\Accountexx Data\...\Tropicana Properties LLC.qbw`. No x86 build needed

T-802 checklist (human, on the server, as the auto-logon account):
- [ ] `deploy\hermes\.env` and `provider.env` filled (README-hermes.md §2); `docker compose config --quiet` prints nothing
- [ ] `.\deploy\start-all.ps1 -WhatIf` → exit 0, lists the steps, changes nothing
- [ ] `.\deploy\start-all.ps1` (add `-DockerMode Desktop` on Windows 10/11) → exit 0; `docker port qbautopost-hermes` shows `127.0.0.1:8642` only; `/health/hermes` 200; `logs\start-all-yyyyMMdd.log` written and holds no key
- [ ] Run it again → "The API already runs … not starting another", exit 0
- [ ] Stop the container (`docker compose down`), stop the API, run it with `-HermesWaitSeconds 30 -ApiWaitSeconds 30` and Hermes kept down (e.g. rename `.env`) → exit 1 (compose refused) or 2 (API started, Hermes unhealthy) as documented
- [ ] `.\deploy\install-task.ps1 -WhatIf` → prints the task, registers nothing
- [ ] `.\deploy\install-task.ps1` (elevated) → Task Scheduler shows `QbAutopost`: "Run only when user is logged on", at logon of the account, delay 1 minute, not "highest privileges"
- [ ] Sign out and in (or reboot with auto-logon) → the API and Hermes come up without anyone touching the server; `/health/quickbooks` ok with QuickBooks open
- [ ] The API window is in the same session as QuickBooks (a COM call works: `scripts\qb-server-check.ps1 -Step health`)

T-803 checklist (human, shadow week; `DryRunDefault=true`, nothing is posted):
- [ ] T-609 and T-802 done on the server; `/health/quickbooks` and `/health/hermes` ok; `POST /qb/sync-lists` run, `missingInRules` empty
- [ ] Pick five real job folders (different banks/cards, at least one PDF statement and one with invoices) that a person is entering by hand as usual
- [ ] For each: `scripts\qb-server-check.ps1 -Step dryrun -Folder <folder>` → `ready` (or `failed` with a reason worth a rule/fix)
- [ ] After the manual entry: export the same period's transactions from QuickBooks to a CSV with `Date,Amount,Payee,Account[,Source]` and run `scripts\shadow-diff.ps1 -Analysis <folder>\output\analysis.json -Manual <csv>`
- [ ] For each disagreement decide: tool wrong → teach a rule (`POST /rules/alias`, `POST /rules/account`) or log a defect; person wrong → note it; held line → teach a rule if the answer is clear
- [ ] After teaching, run the dry run again (same `-Step dryrun`; `force=true` is only needed once a job is in the ledger) and re-diff
- [ ] Record each run in the log below; the week ends after **two consecutive runs with zero disagreements** (plan §6)
- [ ] Check `output\hermes\` and the logs of one job hold no keys

**D-2** two validation endpoints (company file, transaction data) · **D-3** `/api/v1` + an OpenAPI document, reference

Fixed by deriving the status **once** from the counts `Finish` already computes, so four exits cannot disagree, rather than patching each branch. One deliberate departure from the spec's literal "failed (nothing posted)": a **skipped** row does not fail a batch, because G4 skips what QuickBooks already holds, so an all-duplicate re-send posted nothing *because nothing needed posting* - only a **held** row or an `error` makes an empty batch `failed`, and `Should_ReportPosted_When_EveryRowWasAlreadyInQuickBooks` pins that carve-out (it passed when written, which is why it is named as a guard rather than offered as evidence). **One existing assertion changed and is named rather than buried**: `Should_WriteTheRequestBeforeAnythingIsSent_When_QuickBooksFails` asserted `partial` - it had encoded the defect; its ordering and empty-ledger assertions, which are what the test is for, are untouched. The `MaxAgeDays` widening is an **owner decision**, and the honest cost is recorded: the date gate was the only thing between a caller and a posting into a closed period, and on this server it no longer is. The C# default in `DirectLimits` stays **730**, so only the shipped file leans. **The folder path (`JobPipeline`) was not examined** - if it shares the shape, this task did not find it |

Two defects, one symptom. The **path** was written for the project folder while the content root is `AppContext.BaseDirectory` (T-901, deliberate - session-13's published exe read the wrong `appsettings.json` entirely), so `../../samples/rules.json` landed in `bin\`. The **failure** was ungracious, which is the part worth fixing: `Rules.Load` let a raw IO exception escape into a bare `500` with no problem details, in a system that **already knew** - `StartupLog` warns and `/health/ready` reports `rules: false`. Running without the file is an allowed state, so the answer is `503` (the server cannot do this work), never a refusal to start. Checked **in `Rules.Load`**, because five call sites read that file and fixing the endpoint that was noticed would have left four throwing - the folder-path test pins that. **Typed, not `catch (IOException)`**: a missing folder and a missing file raise different exceptions and neither is distinguishable from a failing disk, so the handler matches one type and returns `false` for everything else. The detail **names the resolved path**, asserted by its own test, because the whole defect was a path resolving somewhere nobody expected and no message said where. Development is fixed by **copying the file beside the executable in Debug only** rather than counting `../` - the right depth differs between Debug, Release and publish, so a literal path would be correct in exactly one of them; `CopyToPublishDirectory="Never"` keeps fictional sample accounts off a real server. **Corrupt or empty rules still `500`** - same class, deliberately not fixed, Q-73 |

The document claimed `creditCardCharge`, `creditCardCredit` and `transfer`; the enum is `Check CcCharge CcCredit Deposit Skip`, so three of the five values it offered a reader **do not exist**. T-913's drift tests compared routes, summaries, response schemas and scopes - everything except the one part of the document a caller copies literally into a request body. The new test reflects over every enum type in `QbAutopost.Core` and compares it with any schema of the same name, so `JobStatus` is covered by the same assertion and a future enum schema is covered the day it is added; it compares by value rather than order, and asserts at least two schemas were actually compared so a rename cannot make it pass by matching nothing. **Not fixed here, deliberately**: a body that fails to bind still escapes as an unhandled exception instead of RFC 7807 problem details - same class as T-920's corrupt-rules `500`, recorded as Q-79 |

- [ ] `GET /api/v1/health/sdk` with QuickBooks **closed** → answers (does not hang, does not raise the certificate dialog); `requestProcessor.registered` true, `canOpenConnection` true, `quickBooks.running` false
- [ ] Same call with QuickBooks **open** on the test company → `quickBooks.running` true, `name` `QBW.EXE`, `bitness` `x64`, `bitnessMatch` true, `ok` true
- [ ] `processBitness` reads `x64` (confirms T-609 from inside the app rather than from the install folder)
- [ ] The call opens **no** company file: the QuickBooks title bar does not change and no new certificate prompt appears
- [ ] Two calls in a row both answer within a second (the STA thread is created and released each time)
- [ ] With the x86 build of the package (if still to hand): `bitnessMatch` false, `ok` false, message names both bitnesses
- [ ] `qbXmlVersion` echoes `QuickBooks:QbXmlVersion` from `appsettings.json`
- [ ] Log line `QuickBooks SDK health: …` present; no company file path and no key in it

GREEN); `OpenApiDriftTests`, `V2ScopeMatrixTests`, `V1ContractPinTests`, `V2ReadTests` unedited and green; `dotnet build -warnaserror` and `dotnet test` green (counts in the Session log); Part B: 52 new Api tests + 3 Core tests (RED: 42 Api failing with the route closed to admin and the size rule and v2 health missing, 3 Core failing; GREEN), through the real `HermesClient` over `FakeOpenRouterHandler`; `V1ContractPinTests`, `OpenApiV2DriftTests`, `V2RoutingTests` green; `dotnet build -warnaserror` 0 warnings; `dotnet test` green: Core 1228, Api 1476, Data 470 (1 skipped) | done | Ruling R2 applies to `ListSameFile`. Part A: summary route (scope `requests:read`) checks `from`/`to` (required instants with an offset), compare pair (both or neither), `to > from`, at most 366 days, `bucket` day/week, `tz` (default `UTC`), each once, else `400 invalid-request`; `range` echoes from/to rendered in `tz` with offset, `tz`, `bucket`; money via `MoneyJsonConverter`: never rounded; stored amounts have 2 decimals, so 2 are written (a value with more keeps them; never double). A sent `tz` that is blank or space-padded is `400` on the summary and the list (never trimmed, never read as UTC); `DateBounds` instants end at `\z`, so a trailing newline is refused. Test assemblies Api.Tests and Data.Tests pin invariant cultures in a `[ModuleInitializer]`. `/dashboard/summary` is named in `ScopeFor` under `/api/v2` only (GET → `requests:read`, else admin), like the audit feed; `/api/v1/dashboard/summary` and the flat form keep the admin fall-through (pinned). Ruling R12: `V2ScopeMatrixTests` edited only to add `/dashboard/summary` to its v2-only exception list. List: `tz` + `postedFrom`/`postedTo` + instants in the six date parameters via `DateBounds.TryParse`; without `tz` a plain created/received date still fills the old `DateOnly` fields (behaviour unchanged), a plain posted date is a UTC day; `sort=posted`/`-posted`. Ruling R4 (Q-112): `InvariantGlobalization=false` on Windows for Api, Api.Tests, Data.Tests; Program pins default thread cultures to invariant and warns once if `Asia/Kolkata` does not resolve. Ruling R9 applied to spec §4.2. Part B: `POST /api/v2/statement-extractions` (signed-in user with `requests:submit`; API keys 403). Checks in the 6.1 order; the multipart body is read into memory with `MultipartReader`, capped at `Extraction:MaxBytes` for the file and `MaxBytes + 64 KiB` for the body, never to disk. `RequestSizeMiddleware` gives only that route (POST, `/api/v2` only) its own limit and `413 file-too-large`; Kestrel's limit is the larger of the two; every other route keeps 2 MB and today's problem (pinned). Model call: `IHermesClient` (`HermesTask.Extraction`, `AuditDir: null`, `ResponseFormat("bank_statement", schema, Strict: true)`, `Extraction:Model`), whole call bounded by `Extraction:TimeoutSeconds` (504), client disconnect cancels it; invalid twice or any HTTP/network error is 502 (the client never retries HTTP errors). Strict mode sent; whether OpenRouter accepts it for the model is still Q-108, for the server check (T-1215). Expiry runs first on every successful extraction; duplicates use `SavedRequestIntake.RowFingerprintsOf` (the save path's reader) and the lookback window. One log line per extraction (user id, size, pages, model, prompt version, ms, rows, outcome). Prompt file SHA-256 pinned with `2026-10-28.api-1` (LF bytes). `GET /api/v2/health` added: v1 body + `model` + `extraction` (Q-113). Ruling R11 applied. A reply holding U+0000 is 502, nothing stored (Q-114) |
